package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class AccessAuditReportForm extends ReportWindow {

    public AccessAuditReportForm(UserAccount account) {
        super(
            "Access Activity and Security Audit Report",
            "Review access attempts, affected resources and outcomes.",
            "SELECT "
            + "l.log_id AS 'Log ID', "
            + "u.full_name AS 'User', "
            + "o.operation_name AS Operation, "
            + "r.resource_name AS Resource, "
            + "l.target_record AS 'Target Record', "
            + "l.action_time AS 'Action Time', "
            + "l.result AS Result "
            + "FROM Access_Logs l "
            + "JOIN Users u ON u.user_id = l.user_id "
            + "JOIN Operations o "
            + "ON o.operation_id = l.operation_id "
            + "JOIN Resources r "
            + "ON r.resource_id = l.resource_id "
            + "ORDER BY l.action_time DESC"
        );
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class AccessLog {

    private int logId;
    private int userId;
    private int sessionId;
    private int operationId;
    private int resourceId;
    private String targetRecord;
    private String actionTime;
    private String result;

    public AccessLog() {
    }

    public AccessLog(int logId, int userId, int sessionId, int operationId,
                     int resourceId, String targetRecord, String actionTime,
                     String result) {
        this.logId = logId;
        this.userId = userId;
        this.sessionId = sessionId;
        this.operationId = operationId;
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
        this.targetRecord = targetRecord;
        this.actionTime = actionTime;
        this.result = result;
    }

    public int getLogId() {
        return logId;
    }

    public void setLogId(int logId) {
        this.logId = logId;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public int getSessionId() {
        return sessionId;
    }

    public void setSessionId(int sessionId) {
        this.sessionId = sessionId;
    }

    public int getOperationId() {
        return operationId;
    }

    public void setOperationId(int operationId) {
        this.operationId = operationId;
    }

    public int getResourceId() {
        return resourceId;
    }

    public void setResourceId(int resourceId) {
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
    }

    public String getTargetRecord() {
        return targetRecord;
    }

    public void setTargetRecord(String targetRecord) {
        this.targetRecord = targetRecord;
    }

    public String getActionTime() {
        return actionTime;
    }

    public void setActionTime(String actionTime) {
        this.actionTime = actionTime;
    }

    public String getResult() {
        return result;
    }

    public void setResult(String result) {
        this.result = result;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class AccessLogDAO {

    public List<AccessLog> getAllAccessLogs() {

        List<AccessLog> accessLogs = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT log_id, user_id, session_id, operation_id, "
                   + "resource_id, target_record, action_time, result "
                   + "FROM Access_Logs";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                AccessLog accessLog = new AccessLog(
                        resultSet.getInt("log_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("session_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("operation_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("resource_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("target_record"),
                        resultSet.getString("action_time"),
                        resultSet.getString("result")
                );

                accessLogs.add(accessLog);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving access logs: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return accessLogs;
    }
    
        public boolean addAccessLog(int userId,
                                int sessionId,
                                int operationId,
                                int resourceId,
                                String targetRecord,
                                String result) {

        String sql = "INSERT INTO Access_Logs "
                   + "(user_id, session_id, operation_id, resource_id, "
                   + "target_record, action_time, result) "
                   + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, NOW(), ?)";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);
            statement.setInt(2, sessionId);
            statement.setInt(3, operationId);
            statement.setInt(4, resourceId);
            statement.setString(5, targetRecord);
            statement.setString(6, result);

            int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

            return rowsInserted > 0;

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error adding access log: "
                    + e.getMessage());
            return false;
        }
    }
}

}
    
    private void loadAccessLogs() {

    AccessLogDAO accessLogDAO = new AccessLogDAO();

    java.util.List<AccessLog> accessLogs =
            accessLogDAO.getAllAccessLogs();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) accessLogsTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (AccessLog accessLog : accessLogs) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            accessLog.getLogId(),
            accessLog.getUserId(),
            accessLog.getSessionId(),
            accessLog.getOperationId(),
            accessLog.getResourceId(),
            accessLog.getTargetRecord(),
            accessLog.getActionTime(),
            accessLog.getResult()
        });
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

import java.util.List;

public class AccessPermissionManagementSystem {

    public static void main(String[] args) {

        LoginDAO loginDAO = new LoginDAO();

        String username = "";
        String passwordHash = "";

        UserAccount account =
                loginDAO.login(username, passwordHash);

        if (account != null) {

            System.out.println("Login successful.");
            System.out.println("Welcome, " + account.getUsername());

            RoleCheckDAO roleCheckDAO = new RoleCheckDAO();

            List<Role> roles =
                    roleCheckDAO.getUserRoles(account.getUserId());

            System.out.println("Roles assigned to "
                    + account.getUsername() + ":");

            for (Role role : roles) {
                System.out.println("- " + role.getRoleName());
            }

            PermissionCheckDAO permissionCheckDAO =
                    new PermissionCheckDAO();

            // Test: operation_id = 1, resource_id = 1
            boolean allowed =
                    permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                            account.getUserId(),
                            1,
                            1
                    );

            if (allowed) {
                System.out.println(
                        "Permission granted for operation 1 on resource 1."
                );
            } else {
                System.out.println(
                        "Permission denied for operation 1 on resource 1."
                );
            }

        } else {

            System.out.println("Login failed.");
        }
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class AccessReviewDAO {

    public List<String[]> getPermissionUsageReview() {

        List<String[]> reviewRecords = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql =
                "SELECT "
                + "u.user_id, "
                + "u.full_name, "
                + "r.role_id, "
                + "r.role_name, "
                + "p.permission_id, "
                + "o.operation_name, "
                + "res.resource_name, "
                + "p.description AS permission_description, "
                + "MAX( "
                + "    CASE "
                + "        WHEN al.result = 'ALLOWED' "
                + "        THEN al.action_time "
                + "        ELSE NULL "
                + "    END "
                + ") AS last_used, "
                + "CASE "
                + "    WHEN MAX( "
                + "        CASE "
                + "            WHEN al.result = 'ALLOWED' "
                + "            THEN al.action_time "
                + "            ELSE NULL "
                + "        END "
                + "    ) IS NULL "
                + "    THEN 'UNUSED' "
                + "    ELSE 'USED' "
                + "END AS usage_status "
                + "FROM Users u "
                + "JOIN User_Roles ur "
                + "    ON u.user_id = ur.user_id "
                + "JOIN Roles r "
                + "    ON ur.role_id = r.role_id "
                + "JOIN Role_Permissions rp "
                + "    ON r.role_id = rp.role_id "
                + "JOIN Permissions p "
                + "    ON rp.permission_id = p.permission_id "
                + "JOIN Operations o "
                + "    ON p.operation_id = o.operation_id "
                + "JOIN Resources res "
                + "    ON p.resource_id = res.resource_id "
                + "LEFT JOIN Access_Logs al "
                + "    ON al.user_id = u.user_id "
                + "    AND al.operation_id = p.operation_id "
                + "    AND al.resource_id = p.resource_id "
                + "    AND al.action_time >= DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 6 MONTH) "
                + "GROUP BY "
                + "u.user_id, "
                + "u.full_name, "
                + "r.role_id, "
                + "r.role_name, "
                + "p.permission_id, "
                + "o.operation_name, "
                + "res.resource_name, "
                + "p.description "
                + "ORDER BY "
                + "u.full_name, "
                + "r.role_name, "
                + "usage_status, "
                + "o.operation_name, "
                + "res.resource_name";

        try (Connection connection =
                     DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet =
                     statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                String[] record = new String[10];

                record[0] =
                        String.valueOf(
                                resultSet.getInt("user_id")
                        );

                record[1] =
                        resultSet.getString("full_name");

                record[2] =
                        String.valueOf(
                                resultSet.getInt("role_id")
                        );

                record[3] =
                        resultSet.getString("role_name");

                record[4] =
                        String.valueOf(
                                resultSet.getInt("permission_id")
                        );

                record[5] =
                        resultSet.getString("operation_name");

                record[6] =
                        resultSet.getString("resource_name");

                record[7] =
                        resultSet.getString(
                                "permission_description"
                        );

                record[8] =
                        resultSet.getString("last_used");

                record[9] =
                        resultSet.getString("usage_status");

                reviewRecords.add(record);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {

            System.out.println(
                    "Error retrieving permission usage review: "
                    + e.getMessage()
            );
        }

        return reviewRecords;
    }
    
    public boolean logAccessRemoval(
        int userId,
        int sessionId,
        String targetRecord) {

    String sql =
            "INSERT INTO Access_Logs "
            + "(user_id, session_id, operation_id, resource_id, "
            + "target_record, action_time, result) "
            + "SELECT ?, ?, o.operation_id, r.resource_id, "
            + "?, NOW(), 'ALLOWED' "
            + "FROM Operations o "
            + "JOIN Resources r "
            + "ON r.resource_name = 'Access Reviews' "
            + "WHERE o.operation_name = 'MANAGE'";

    try (Connection connection =
                 DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, userId);
        statement.setInt(2, sessionId);
        statement.setString(3, targetRecord);

        int rowsInserted =
                statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsInserted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println(
                "Error logging access removal: "
                + e.getMessage()
        );

        return false;
    }
}
    
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */
public class AccessReviewForm extends javax.swing.JFrame {

    /**
     * Creates new form AccessReviewForm
     */
    private UserAccount loggedInAccount;

    private AccessReviewDAO accessReviewDAO =
        new AccessReviewDAO();

    private PermissionCheckDAO permissionCheckDAO =
        new PermissionCheckDAO();

    private java.util.List<String[]> reviewRecords =
        new java.util.ArrayList<>(); 
    private UserRoleDAO userRoleDAO =
        new UserRoleDAO();
    public AccessReviewForm(UserAccount account) {

    initComponents();

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    if (this.loggedInAccount != null) {

        int userId =
                this.loggedInAccount.getUserId();

        boolean allowed =
                permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                        userId,
                        "VIEW",
                        "Access Reviews"
                );

        if (!allowed) {

            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Access denied. You do not have permission to view Access Reviews.",
                    "Permission Denied",
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
            );

            this.dispose();

            return;
        }
        
        boolean canManageAccessReviews =
        permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                userId,
                "MANAGE",
                "Access Reviews"
        );

       removeAccessButton.setEnabled(
        canManageAccessReviews
        );
    }

    loadReviewRecords();
}
    
    private void loadReviewRecords() {

    reviewRecords =
            accessReviewDAO.getPermissionUsageReview();

    displayRecords(reviewRecords);
}
    
    private void displayRecords(
        java.util.List<String[]> records) {

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            new javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel(
                    new Object[]{
                        "User ID",
                        "Full Name",
                        "Role",
                        "Permission ID",
                        "Operation",
                        "Resource",
                        "Description",
                        "Last Used",
                        "Status"
                    },
                    0
            ) {

        @Override
        public boolean isCellEditable(
                int row,
                int column) {

            return false;
        }
    };

    for (String[] record : records) {

        model.addRow(
                new Object[]{
                    record[0],
                    record[1],
                    record[3],
                    record[4],
                    record[5],
                    record[6],
                    record[7],
                    record[8] == null
                            ? "Never used"
                            : record[8],
                    record[9]
                }
        );
    }

    accessReviewTable.setModel(model);
}
    
    private void displayUnusedRecords() {

    java.util.List<String[]> unusedRecords =
            new java.util.ArrayList<>();

    for (String[] record : reviewRecords) {

        if ("UNUSED".equals(record[9])) {

            unusedRecords.add(record);
        }
    }

    displayRecords(unusedRecords);
}
    
    private String[] findSelectedReviewRecord(
        int modelRow) {

    if (modelRow < 0
            || modelRow >= reviewRecords.size()) {

        return null;
    }

    String userId =
            String.valueOf(
                    accessReviewTable
                            .getModel()
                            .getValueAt(modelRow, 0)
            );

    String fullName =
            String.valueOf(
                    accessReviewTable
                            .getModel()
                            .getValueAt(modelRow, 1)
            );

    String roleName =
            String.valueOf(
                    accessReviewTable
                            .getModel()
                            .getValueAt(modelRow, 2)
            );

    String permissionId =
            String.valueOf(
                    accessReviewTable
                            .getModel()
                            .getValueAt(modelRow, 3)
            );

    for (String[] record : reviewRecords) {

        if (record[0].equals(userId)
                && record[1].equals(fullName)
                && record[3].equals(roleName)
                && record[4].equals(permissionId)) {

            return record;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

    private void removeAccessButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_removeAccessButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (loggedInAccount == null) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "No logged-in account was found.",
                "Access Error",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int reviewerUserId =
            loggedInAccount.getUserId();

    boolean allowed =
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    reviewerUserId,
                    "MANAGE",
                    "Access Reviews"
            );

    if (!allowed) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Access denied. You do not have permission to manage Access Reviews.",
                "Permission Denied",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int selectedRow =
            accessReviewTable.getSelectedRow();

    if (selectedRow == -1) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please select an access review record first.",
                "No Selection",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int modelRow =
            accessReviewTable.convertRowIndexToModel(
                    selectedRow
            );

    String status =
            String.valueOf(
                    accessReviewTable
                            .getModel()
                            .getValueAt(modelRow, 8)
            );

    if (!"UNUSED".equals(status)) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "This permission is marked as USED.\n"
                + "Only UNUSED access should be considered for removal.",
                "Access Already Used",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    String[] selectedRecord =
            findSelectedReviewRecord(modelRow);

    if (selectedRecord == null) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The selected review record could not be found.",
                "Review Error",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int userId =
            Integer.parseInt(selectedRecord[0]);

    String fullName =
            selectedRecord[1];

    int roleId =
            Integer.parseInt(selectedRecord[2]);

    String roleName =
            selectedRecord[3];

    String operationName =
            selectedRecord[5];

    String resourceName =
            selectedRecord[6];

    int confirmation =
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                    this,
                    "You are about to remove the role assignment.\n\n"
                    + "User: " + fullName + "\n"
                    + "Role: " + roleName + "\n"
                    + "Unused permission: "
                    + operationName + " "
                    + resourceName + "\n\n"
                    + "WARNING:\n"
                    + "Removing this role will remove ALL permissions "
                    + "that this user inherits from this role.\n\n"
                    + "Do you want to continue?",
                    "Confirm Access Removal",
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
            );

    if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

        return;
    }

    boolean removed =
            userRoleDAO.removeUserRole(
                    userId,
                    roleId
            );

    if (removed) {

    int sessionId =
            new SessionDAO().getActiveSessionId(
                    reviewerUserId
            );

    String targetRecord =
            "Removed role '" + roleName
            + "' from user '" + fullName
            + "' after access review";

    boolean logged =
            accessReviewDAO.logAccessRemoval(
                    reviewerUserId,
                    sessionId,
                    targetRecord
            );

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Role access removed successfully.\n\n"
            + "User: " + fullName + "\n"
            + "Role: " + roleName
            + (logged
                    ? "\n\nAccess-removal action recorded."
                    : "\n\nWarning: removal succeeded, "
                    + "but the audit entry could not be recorded."),
            "Access Removed",
            logged
                    ? javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
                    : javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
    );

    loadReviewRecords();

    } else {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The role assignment could not be removed.",
                "Removal Failed",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );
    }
    }//GEN-LAST:event_removeAccessButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String operationName =
        operationNameField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (operationName.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter an operation name."
    );

    return;
}

Operation operation =
        new Operation();

operation.setOperationName(operationName);
operation.setDescription(description);

OperationDAO operationDAO =
        new OperationDAO();

boolean success =
        operationDAO.addOperation(operation);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation added successfully."
    );

    OperationsForm operationsForm =
            new OperationsForm(loggedInAccount);

    operationsForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation could not be added."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void clearButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String operationText =
        operationIdField.getText().trim();

String resourceText =
        resourceIdField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (operationText.isEmpty()
        || resourceText.isEmpty()
        || description.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter operation ID, resource ID, and description."
    );

    return;
}

int operationId;
int resourceId;

try {

    operationId = Integer.parseInt(operationText);
    resourceId = Integer.parseInt(resourceText);

} catch (NumberFormatException e) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation ID and Resource ID must be numbers."
    );

    return;
}

Permission permission = new Permission();

permission.setOperationId(operationId);
permission.setResourceId(resourceId);
permission.setDescription(description);

PermissionDAO permissionDAO =
        new PermissionDAO();

boolean success =
        permissionDAO.addPermission(permission);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Permission added successfully."
    );

    PermissionsForm permissionsForm =
            new PermissionsForm(loggedInAccount);

    permissionsForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to add permission."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void clearButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        operationIdField.setText("");

        resourceIdField.setText("");

        descriptionField.setText("");
    }//GEN-LAST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed

    private void cancelButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:

        new PermissionsForm(loggedInAccount);

        permissionsForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
        
    }//GEN-LAST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        String resourceName =
        resourceNameField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (resourceName.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter a resource name."
    );

    return;
}

Resource resource = new Resource();

resource.setResourceName(resourceName);
resource.setDescription(description);

ResourceDAO resourceDAO = new ResourceDAO();

boolean success =
        resourceDAO.addResource(resource);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Resource added successfully."
    );

    ResourcesForm resourcesForm =
            new ResourcesForm(loggedInAccount);

    resourcesForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Resource could not be added."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String roleName = roleNameField.getText().trim();
String description = descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (roleName.isEmpty() || description.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter role name and description."
    );

    return;
}

Role role = new Role();

role.setRoleName(roleName);
role.setDescription(description);

RoleDAO roleDAO = new RoleDAO();

boolean success = roleDAO.addRole(role);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Role added successfully."
    );

    RolesForm rolesForm =
            new RolesForm(loggedInAccount);

    rolesForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to add role."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

        emailField.setText("");

        usernameField.setText("");

        passwordField.setText("");

        statusComboBox.setSelectedIndex(0);
    }//GEN-LAST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String fullName =
            fullNameField.getText().trim();

    String email =
            emailField.getText().trim();

    String username =
            usernameField.getText().trim();

    String password =
            new String(
                    passwordField.getPassword()
            );

    String status =
            statusComboBox
                    .getSelectedItem()
                    .toString();


    // Check required fields

    if (fullName.isEmpty()
            || email.isEmpty()
            || username.isEmpty()
            || password.isEmpty()) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please enter full name, email, username and password.",
                "Missing Information",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }


    // Check password length

    if (password.length() < 6) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Password must contain at least 6 characters.",
                "Invalid Password",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }


    // Create User record

    User user =
            new User();

    user.setFullName(fullName);

    user.setEmail(email);

    user.setStatus(status);


    UserDAO userDAO =
            new UserDAO();

    int userId =
            userDAO.addUser(user);


    if (userId == -1) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Failed to create the user.",
                "Operation Failed",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }


    // Create login account

    UserAccountDAO userAccountDAO =
            new UserAccountDAO();

    boolean accountCreated =
            userAccountDAO.createAccount(
                    userId,
                    username,
                    password,
                    status.equalsIgnoreCase("ACTIVE")
                            ? "ACTIVE"
                            : "DISABLED"
            );


    if (accountCreated) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "User and login account created successfully."
        );

        UsersForm usersForm =
                new UsersForm(
                        loggedInAccount
                );

        usersForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();

    } else {

        /*
         * The User record was created,
         * but the login account failed.
         *
         * Remove the User record so we
         * do not leave an incomplete user.
         */

        userDAO.deleteUser(userId);

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The user was created, but the login account "
                + "could not be created.\n"
                + "The user record has been rolled back.",
                "Account Creation Failed",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );
    }
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private final PermissionCheckDAO permissionCheckDAO =
        new PermissionCheckDAO();

    public DashboardForm(UserAccount account) {

    initComponents();

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    loadUserInformation();
    loadUserPermissions();
}

    private void loadUserInformation() {

        usernameLabel.setText(
                "Username: " + loggedInAccount.getUsername()
        );

        RoleCheckDAO roleCheckDAO = new RoleCheckDAO();

        java.util.List<Role> roles
                = roleCheckDAO.getUserRoles(
                        loggedInAccount.getUserId()
                );

        StringBuilder roleText
                = new StringBuilder("Roles: ");

        for (Role role : roles) {

            roleText.append(role.getRoleName());
            roleText.append("  ");
        }

        rolesLabel.setText(roleText.toString());
    }
    
    private void loadUserPermissions() {

    int userId = loggedInAccount.getUserId();

    usersMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Users"
            )
    );

    rolesMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Roles"
            )
    );

    permissionsMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Permissions"
            )
    );

    resourcesMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Resources"
            )
    );

    operationsMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Operations"
            )
    );

    sessionsMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Sessions"
            )
    );

    accessLogsMenuItem.setVisible(
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Access Logs"
            )
    );
    
    boolean canViewReports = permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        userId,
        "VIEW",
        "Reports"
    );

    userAccountsReportMenuItem.setVisible(canViewReports);
    roleAssignmentsReportMenuItem.setVisible(canViewReports);
    rolePermissionsReportMenuItem.setVisible(canViewReports);
    loginSessionsReportMenuItem.setVisible(canViewReports);
    accessAuditReportMenuItem.setVisible(canViewReports);
    
    accessReviewMenuItem.setVisible(
        permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                userId,
                "VIEW",
                "Access Reviews"
        )
    );
}

    private void logoutMenuItemActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_logoutMenuItemActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        SessionDAO sessionDAO = new SessionDAO();

int sessionId =
        sessionDAO.getActiveSessionId(
                loggedInAccount.getUserId()
        );

if (sessionId != 0) {

    boolean sessionClosed =
            sessionDAO.closeSession(sessionId);

    if (!sessionClosed) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Warning: The session could not be closed.",
                "Logout Warning",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );
    }
}

LoginForm loginForm = new LoginForm();

loginForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_logoutMenuItemActionPerformed

    private void userAccountsReportMenuItemActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_userAccountsReportMenuItemActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        int userId = loggedInAccount.getUserId();

        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
            userId, "VIEW", "Reports")) {
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "You do not have permission to view reports.",
                "Access Denied",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE);
        return;
    }

    new UserAccountsReportForm(loggedInAccount).setVisible(true);
    }//GEN-LAST:event_userAccountsReportMenuItemActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public class DatabaseConnection {

    private static final String URL =
            "jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/access_permission_management_system";

    private static final String USER = "root";
    private static final String PASSWORD = "";

    public static Connection getConnection() {
        try {
            return DriverManager.getConnection(URL, USER, PASSWORD);
        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Database connection failed: " + e.getMessage());
            return null;
        }
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class Department {

    private int departmentId;
    private String departmentName;
    private String description;

    public Department() {
    }

    public Department(int departmentId, String departmentName, String description) {
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
        this.departmentName = departmentName;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public int getDepartmentId() {
        return departmentId;
    }

    public void setDepartmentId(int departmentId) {
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
    }

    public String getDepartmentName() {
        return departmentName;
    }

    public void setDepartmentName(String departmentName) {
        this.departmentName = departmentName;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public void setDescription(String description) {
        this.description = description;
    }
}

 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class DepartmentDAO {

    public List<Department> getAllDepartments() {

        List<Department> departments = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT department_id, department_name, description "
                   + "FROM Departments";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Department department = new Department(
                        resultSet.getInt("department_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("department_name"),
                        resultSet.getString("description")
                );

                departments.add(department);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving departments: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return departments;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class DepartmentRole {

    private int departmentId;
    private int roleId;

    public DepartmentRole() {
    }

    public DepartmentRole(int departmentId, int roleId) {
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
        this.roleId = roleId;
    }

    public int getDepartmentId() {
        return departmentId;
    }

    public void setDepartmentId(int departmentId) {
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
    }

    public int getRoleId() {
        return roleId;
    }

    public void setRoleId(int roleId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
    }
}

 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class DepartmentRoleDAO {

    public List<DepartmentRole> getAllDepartmentRoles() {

        List<DepartmentRole> departmentRoles = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT department_id, role_id FROM Department_Roles";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                DepartmentRole departmentRole = new DepartmentRole(
                        resultSet.getInt("department_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id")
                );

                departmentRoles.add(departmentRole);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving department roles: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return departmentRoles;
    }
}

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String operationName =
        operationNameField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (operationName.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter an operation name."
    );

    return;
}

Operation operation =
        new Operation();

operation.setOperationId(operationId);
operation.setOperationName(operationName);
operation.setDescription(description);

OperationDAO operationDAO =
        new OperationDAO();

boolean success =
        operationDAO.updateOperation(operation);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation updated successfully."
    );

    OperationsForm operationsForm =
            new OperationsForm(loggedInAccount);

    operationsForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation could not be updated."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void clearButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        operationNameField.setText("");
        descriptionField.setText("");
        operationNameField.requestFocus();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed

    private void cancelButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

        new OperationsForm(loggedInAccount);

        operationsForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String operationText =
        operationIdField.getText().trim();

String resourceText =
        resourceIdField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (operationText.isEmpty()
        || resourceText.isEmpty()
        || description.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter operation ID, resource ID, and description."
    );

    return;
}

int operationId;
int resourceId;

try {

    operationId = Integer.parseInt(operationText);
    resourceId = Integer.parseInt(resourceText);

} catch (NumberFormatException e) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation ID and Resource ID must be numbers."
    );

    return;
}

permission.setOperationId(operationId);
permission.setResourceId(resourceId);
permission.setDescription(description);

PermissionDAO permissionDAO =
        new PermissionDAO();

boolean success =
        permissionDAO.updatePermission(permission);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Permission updated successfully."
    );

    PermissionsForm permissionsForm =
            new PermissionsForm(loggedInAccount);

    permissionsForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to update permission."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void cancelButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        PermissionsForm permissionsForm =
        new PermissionsForm(loggedInAccount);

        permissionsForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String resourceName =
        resourceNameField.getText().trim();

String description =
        descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (resourceName.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter a resource name."
    );

    return;
}

Resource resource = new Resource();

resource.setResourceId(resourceId);
resource.setResourceName(resourceName);
resource.setDescription(description);

ResourceDAO resourceDAO = new ResourceDAO();

boolean success =
        resourceDAO.updateResource(resource);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Resource updated successfully."
    );

    ResourcesForm resourcesForm =
            new ResourcesForm(loggedInAccount);

    resourcesForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Resource could not be updated."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void clearButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        resourceNameField.setText("");
        descriptionField.setText("");
        resourceNameField.requestFocus();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String roleName = roleNameField.getText().trim();
String description = descriptionField.getText().trim();

if (roleName.isEmpty() || description.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter role name and description."
    );

    return;
}

role.setRoleName(roleName);
role.setDescription(description);

RoleDAO roleDAO = new RoleDAO();

boolean success = roleDAO.updateRole(role);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Role updated successfully."
    );

    RolesForm rolesForm =
            new RolesForm(loggedInAccount);

    rolesForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to update role."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void saveButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String fullName = fullNameField.getText().trim();
String email = emailField.getText().trim();
String status =
        statusComboBox.getSelectedItem().toString();

if (fullName.isEmpty() || email.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter full name and email."
    );

    return;
}

user.setFullName(fullName);
user.setEmail(email);
user.setStatus(status);

UserDAO userDAO = new UserDAO();

boolean success = userDAO.updateUser(user);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "User updated successfully."
    );

    UsersForm usersForm =
            new UsersForm(loggedInAccount);

    usersForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to update user."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_saveButtonActionPerformed

    private void clearButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        fullNameField.setText("");

        emailField.setText("");

        statusComboBox.setSelectedIndex(0);
    }//GEN-LAST:event_clearButtonActionPerformed

        usersForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_cancelButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class EmployeeRecord {

    private int employeeId;
    private int userId;
    private int departmentId;
    private String jobTitle;
    private String employmentStatus;

    public EmployeeRecord() {
    }

    public EmployeeRecord(int employeeId, int userId, int departmentId,
                          String jobTitle, String employmentStatus) {
        this.employeeId = employeeId;
        this.userId = userId;
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
        this.jobTitle = jobTitle;
        this.employmentStatus = employmentStatus;
    }

    public int getEmployeeId() {
        return employeeId;
    }

    public void setEmployeeId(int employeeId) {
        this.employeeId = employeeId;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public int getDepartmentId() {
        return departmentId;
    }

    public void setDepartmentId(int departmentId) {
        this.departmentId = departmentId;
    }

    public String getJobTitle() {
        return jobTitle;
    }

    public void setJobTitle(String jobTitle) {
        this.jobTitle = jobTitle;
    }

    public String getEmploymentStatus() {
        return employmentStatus;
    }

    public void setEmploymentStatus(String employmentStatus) {
        this.employmentStatus = employmentStatus;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class EmployeeRecordDAO {

    public List<EmployeeRecord> getAllEmployeeRecords() {

        List<EmployeeRecord> employeeRecords = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT employee_id, user_id, department_id, "
                   + "job_title, employment_status "
                   + "FROM Employee_Records";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                EmployeeRecord employeeRecord = new EmployeeRecord(
                        resultSet.getInt("employee_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("department_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("job_title"),
                        resultSet.getString("employment_status")
                );

                employeeRecords.add(employeeRecord);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving employee records: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return employeeRecords;
    }
}
package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public class LoginDAO {

    public UserAccount login(
        String username,
        String password) {

    String passwordHash =
            PasswordUtil.hashPassword(password);

    String sql =
            "SELECT account_id, user_id, username, "
            + "password_hash, account_status, last_login "
            + "FROM User_Accounts "
            + "WHERE username = ? "
            + "AND password_hash = ?";

    try (Connection connection =
                 DatabaseConnection.getConnection();

         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, username);
        statement.setString(2, passwordHash);

        try (ResultSet resultSet =
                     statement.executeQuery()) {

            if (resultSet.next()) {

                String accountStatus =
                        resultSet.getString(
                                "account_status"
                        );

                if (!"ACTIVE".equalsIgnoreCase(
                        accountStatus)) {

                    UserAccount disabledAccount =
                            new UserAccount(
                                    resultSet.getInt("account_id"),
                                    resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                                    resultSet.getString("username"),
                                    resultSet.getString("password_hash"),
                                    resultSet.getString("account_status"),
                                    resultSet.getString("last_login")
                            );

                    return disabledAccount;
                }

                return new UserAccount(
                        resultSet.getInt("account_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("username"),
                        resultSet.getString("password_hash"),
                        resultSet.getString("account_status"),
                        resultSet.getString("last_login")
                );
            }
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println(
                "Login error: "
                + e.getMessage()
        );
    }

    return null;
}
}

    private void loginButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_loginButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        String username = usernameField.getText();
        String password =
        new String(
                passwordField.getPassword()
        );

if (username.isEmpty()
        || password.isEmpty()) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please enter both username and password.",
            "Login",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
    );

    return;
}

LoginDAO loginDAO =
        new LoginDAO();

UserAccount account =
        loginDAO.login(
                username,
                password
        );

if (account != null) {

    if (!"ACTIVE".equalsIgnoreCase(
            account.getAccountStatus())) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Your account is disabled. "
                + "Please contact the administrator.",
                "Login Rejected",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    SessionDAO sessionDAO =
            new SessionDAO();

    sessionDAO.createSession(
            account.getUserId()
    );

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
        this,
        "Welcome, " + account.getUsername() + "!",
        "Login Successful",
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
    );

    DashboardForm dashboardForm =
        new DashboardForm(account);

    dashboardForm.setVisible(true);

    this.dispose();

    } else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Invalid username or password.",
            "Login Failed",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_loginButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class LoginSessionsReportForm extends ReportWindow {

    public LoginSessionsReportForm(UserAccount account) {
        super(
            "Login and Sessions Report",
            "Review login history and sessions that remain open.",
            "SELECT "
            + "s.session_id AS 'Session ID', "
            + "u.full_name AS 'Full Name', "
            + "a.username AS Username, "
            + "s.login_time AS 'Login Time', "
            + "s.logout_time AS 'Logout Time', "
            + "CASE WHEN s.logout_time IS NULL "
            + "THEN 'OPEN' ELSE 'CLOSED' END AS 'Session Status' "
            + "FROM Sessions s "
            + "JOIN Users u ON u.user_id = s.user_id "
            + "LEFT JOIN User_Accounts a "
            + "ON a.user_id = u.user_id "
            + "ORDER BY s.login_time DESC"
        );
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class Operation {

    private int operationId;
    private String operationName;
    private String description;

    public Operation() {
    }

    public Operation(int operationId, String operationName, String description) {
        this.operationId = operationId;
        this.operationName = operationName;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public int getOperationId() {
        return operationId;
    }

    public void setOperationId(int operationId) {
        this.operationId = operationId;
    }

    public String getOperationName() {
        return operationName;
    }

    public void setOperationName(String operationName) {
        this.operationName = operationName;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public void setDescription(String description) {
        this.description = description;
    }
}
/*
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Classes/Class.java to edit this template
 */
package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class OperationDAO {

    public List<Operation> getAllOperations() {

        List<Operation> operations = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT operation_id, operation_name, description "
                   + "FROM Operations";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Operation operation = new Operation(
                        resultSet.getInt("operation_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("operation_name"),
                        resultSet.getString("description")
                );

                operations.add(operation);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving operations: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return operations;
    }
    
    public boolean addOperation(Operation operation) {

    String sql = "INSERT INTO Operations "
               + "(operation_name, description) "
               + "VALUES (?, ?)";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, operation.getOperationName());
        statement.setString(2, operation.getDescription());

        int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsInserted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error adding operation: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}

public boolean updateOperation(Operation operation) {

    String sql = "UPDATE Operations "
               + "SET operation_name = ?, description = ? "
               + "WHERE operation_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, operation.getOperationName());
        statement.setString(2, operation.getDescription());
        statement.setInt(3, operation.getOperationId());

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error updating operation: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}

public boolean deleteOperation(int operationId) {

    String sql = "DELETE FROM Operations "
               + "WHERE operation_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, operationId);

        int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsDeleted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error deleting operation: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    loadOperations();
}
    
    private void loadOperations() {

    OperationDAO operationDAO =
            new OperationDAO();

    java.util.List<Operation> operations =
            operationDAO.getAllOperations();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel)
                    operationsTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (Operation operation : operations) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            operation.getOperationId(),
            operation.getOperationName(),
            operation.getDescription()
        });
    }
}

    private void refreshButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_refreshButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        loadOperations();

        this,
        "Operations refreshed successfully."
);
    }//GEN-LAST:event_refreshButtonActionPerformed

    private void addOperationButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_addOperationButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "CREATE",
        "Operations")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to create operations."
    );

    return;
}

AddOperationForm addOperationForm =
        new AddOperationForm(loggedInAccount);

addOperationForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_addOperationButtonActionPerformed

    private void updateOperationButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_updateOperationButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Operations")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to update operations."
    );

    return;
}

int selectedRow =
        operationsTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select an operation to update."
    );

    return;
}

int operationId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                operationsTable
                        .getValueAt(selectedRow, 0)
                        .toString()
        );

String operationName =
        operationsTable
                .getValueAt(selectedRow, 1)
                .toString();

String description =
        operationsTable
                .getValueAt(selectedRow, 2)
                .toString();

EditOperationForm editOperationForm =
        new EditOperationForm(
                loggedInAccount,
                operationId,
                operationName,
                description
        );

editOperationForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_updateOperationButtonActionPerformed

    private void deleteOperationButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_deleteOperationButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "DELETE",
        "Operations")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to delete operations."
    );

    return;
}

int selectedRow =
        operationsTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select an operation to delete."
    );

    return;
}

int operationId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                operationsTable
                        .getValueAt(selectedRow, 0)
                        .toString()
        );

String operationName =
        operationsTable
                .getValueAt(selectedRow, 1)
                .toString();

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to delete "
                + operationName + "?",
                "Confirm Delete",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

OperationDAO operationDAO =
        new OperationDAO();

boolean success =
        operationDAO.deleteOperation(operationId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Operation deleted successfully."
    );

    loadOperations();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "The operation could not be deleted.\n\n"
            + "It may be referenced by one or more permissions.",
            "Delete Failed",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_deleteOperationButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;

public class PasswordUtil {

    public static String hashPassword(String password) {

        if (password == null) {
            return null;
        }

        try {

            MessageDigest messageDigest =
                    MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");

            byte[] hashBytes =
                    messageDigest.digest(
                            password.getBytes(
                                    StandardCharsets.UTF_8
                            )
                    );

            StringBuilder hash =
                    new StringBuilder();

            for (byte b : hashBytes) {

                hash.append(
                        String.format(
                                "%02x",
                                b
                        )
                );
            }

            return hash.toString();

        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {

            throw new RuntimeException(
                    "SHA-256 algorithm is not available.",
                    e
            );
        }
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class Permission {

    private int permissionId;
    private int operationId;
    private int resourceId;
    private String description;

    public Permission() {
    }

    public Permission(int permissionId, int operationId, int resourceId,
                      String description) {
        this.permissionId = permissionId;
        this.operationId = operationId;
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public int getPermissionId() {
        return permissionId;
    }

    public void setPermissionId(int permissionId) {
        this.permissionId = permissionId;
    }

    public int getOperationId() {
        return operationId;
    }

    public void setOperationId(int operationId) {
        this.operationId = operationId;
    }

    public int getResourceId() {
        return resourceId;
    }

    public void setResourceId(int resourceId) {
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public void setDescription(String description) {
        this.description = description;
    }
}

 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;

public class PermissionCheckDAO {
    
        private AccessLogDAO accessLogDAO = new AccessLogDAO();

        public boolean hasPermission(int userId, int operationId, int resourceId) {

        String sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) "
                   + "FROM User_Roles ur "
                   + "INNER JOIN Role_Permissions rp "
                   + "ON ur.role_id = rp.role_id "
                   + "INNER JOIN Permissions p "
                   + "ON rp.permission_id = p.permission_id "
                   + "WHERE ur.user_id = ? "
                   + "AND p.operation_id = ? "
                   + "AND p.resource_id = ?";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);
            statement.setInt(2, operationId);
            statement.setInt(3, resourceId);

            try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

                if (resultSet.next()) {

                    boolean allowed = resultSet.getInt(1) > 0;

                    int sessionId =
                            new SessionDAO().getActiveSessionId(userId);

                    if (sessionId > 0) {
                        accessLogDAO.addAccessLog(
                                userId,
                                sessionId,
                                operationId,
                                resourceId,
                                null,
                                allowed ? "ALLOWED" : "DENIED"
                        );
                    }

                    return allowed;
                }
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Permission check error: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }
    
        public boolean hasPermission(int userId,
                                 String operationName,
                                 String resourceName) {

        String sql = "SELECT p.operation_id, p.resource_id, COUNT(*) "
                   + "FROM User_Roles ur "
                   + "INNER JOIN Role_Permissions rp "
                   + "ON ur.role_id = rp.role_id "
                   + "INNER JOIN Permissions p "
                   + "ON rp.permission_id = p.permission_id "
                   + "INNER JOIN Operations o "
                   + "ON p.operation_id = o.operation_id "
                   + "INNER JOIN Resources r "
                   + "ON p.resource_id = r.resource_id "
                   + "WHERE ur.user_id = ? "
                   + "AND o.operation_name = ? "
                   + "AND r.resource_name = ? "
                   + "GROUP BY p.operation_id, p.resource_id";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);
            statement.setString(2, operationName);
            statement.setString(3, resourceName);

            try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

                int operationId = 0;
                int resourceId = 0;
                boolean allowed = false;

                if (resultSet.next()) {
                    operationId = resultSet.getInt("operation_id");
                    resourceId = resultSet.getInt("resource_id");
                    allowed = resultSet.getInt(3) > 0;
                } else {

                    String idSql = "SELECT o.operation_id, r.resource_id "
                                 + "FROM Operations o, Resources r "
                                 + "WHERE o.operation_name = ? "
                                 + "AND r.resource_name = ?";

                    try (PreparedStatement idStatement =
                            connection.prepareStatement(idSql)) {

                        idStatement.setString(1, operationName);
                        idStatement.setString(2, resourceName);

                        try (ResultSet idResult =
                                idStatement.executeQuery()) {

                            if (idResult.next()) {
                                operationId =
                                        idResult.getInt("operation_id");
                                resourceId =
                                        idResult.getInt("resource_id");
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                int sessionId =
                        new SessionDAO().getActiveSessionId(userId);

                if (sessionId > 0 && operationId > 0 && resourceId > 0) {
                    accessLogDAO.addAccessLog(
                            userId,
                            sessionId,
                            operationId,
                            resourceId,
                            null,
                            allowed ? "ALLOWED" : "DENIED"
                    );
                }

                return allowed;
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Permission check error: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return false;
    }
}
