package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class PermissionDAO {

    public List<Permission> getAllPermissions() {

        List<Permission> permissions = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT permission_id, operation_id, resource_id, description "
                   + "FROM Permissions";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Permission permission = new Permission(
                        resultSet.getInt("permission_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("operation_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("resource_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("description")
                );

                permissions.add(permission);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving permissions: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return permissions;
    }
    
    public boolean addPermission(Permission permission) {

    String sql = "INSERT INTO Permissions "
               + "(operation_id, resource_id, description) "
               + "VALUES (?, ?, ?)";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, permission.getOperationId());
        statement.setInt(2, permission.getResourceId());
        statement.setString(3, permission.getDescription());

        int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsInserted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error adding permission: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean updatePermission(Permission permission) {

    String sql = "UPDATE Permissions "
               + "SET operation_id = ?, resource_id = ?, description = ? "
               + "WHERE permission_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, permission.getOperationId());
        statement.setInt(2, permission.getResourceId());
        statement.setString(3, permission.getDescription());
        statement.setInt(4, permission.getPermissionId());

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error updating permission: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean deletePermission(int permissionId) {

    String sql = "DELETE FROM Permissions WHERE permission_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, permissionId);

        int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsDeleted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error deleting permission: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    loadPermissions();

    private void loadPermissions() {

    PermissionDAO permissionDAO = new PermissionDAO();

    java.util.List<Permission> permissions =
            permissionDAO.getAllPermissions();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel)
            permissionsTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (Permission permission : permissions) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            permission.getPermissionId(),
            permission.getOperationId(),
            permission.getResourceId(),
            permission.getDescription()
        });
    }
}

    private void backButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_backButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        DashboardForm dashboardForm =
            new DashboardForm(loggedInAccount);

        dashboardForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_backButtonActionPerformed

    private void addPermissionButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_addPermissionButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "CREATE",
        "Permissions")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to create permissions."
    );

    return;
}
        
        AddPermissionForm addPermissionForm =
        new AddPermissionForm(loggedInAccount);

        addPermissionForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_addPermissionButtonActionPerformed

    private void updatePermissionButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_updatePermissionButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Permissions")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to update permissions."
    );

    return;
}
        
        int selectedRow = permissionsTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a permission to update."
    );

    return;
}

int permissionId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                permissionsTable.getValueAt(
                        selectedRow, 0
                ).toString()
        );

int operationId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                permissionsTable.getValueAt(
                        selectedRow, 1
                ).toString()
        );

int resourceId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                permissionsTable.getValueAt(
                        selectedRow, 2
                ).toString()
        );

Permission permission = new Permission();

permission.setPermissionId(permissionId);
permission.setOperationId(operationId);
permission.setResourceId(resourceId);

EditPermissionForm editPermissionForm =
        new EditPermissionForm(
                loggedInAccount,
                permission
        );

editPermissionForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_updatePermissionButtonActionPerformed

    private void deletePermissionButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_deletePermissionButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "DELETE",
        "Permissions")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to delete permissions."
    );

    return;
}
        
        int selectedRow = permissionsTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a permission to delete."
    );

    return;
}

int permissionId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                permissionsTable.getValueAt(
                        selectedRow, 0
                ).toString()
        );

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to delete permission "
                + permissionId + "?",
                "Confirm Delete",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

PermissionDAO permissionDAO =
        new PermissionDAO();

boolean success =
        permissionDAO.deletePermission(permissionId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Permission deleted successfully."
    );

    loadPermissions();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Permission could not be deleted. "
            + "It may be assigned to a role."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_deletePermissionButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Timestamp;
import java.util.Date;

public class ReportDAO {

    public String generateReport(Date fromDate, Date toDate) {

        StringBuilder report = new StringBuilder();

        Timestamp fromTimestamp =
                new Timestamp(fromDate.getTime());

        Timestamp toTimestamp =
                new Timestamp(toDate.getTime());

        /*
         * The To Date is made inclusive by adding one day.
         * Therefore, selecting 2026-10-12 includes the
         * entire day of October 12.
         */
        java.sql.Timestamp endTimestamp;

        try {
            java.util.Calendar calendar =
                    java.util.Calendar.getInstance();

            calendar.setTime(toDate);

            calendar.add(java.util.Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH, 1);

            endTimestamp =
                    new Timestamp(calendar.getTimeInMillis());

        } catch (Exception e) {

            return "Unable to prepare report dates: "
                    + e.getMessage();
        }

        report.append("========================================\n");
        report.append("       ACCESS PERMISSION SYSTEM\n");
        report.append("              REPORT\n");
        report.append("========================================\n\n");

        report.append("REPORT PERIOD\n");
        report.append("----------------------------------------\n");
        report.append("From: ")
              .append(fromDate)
              .append("\n");

        report.append("To:   ")
              .append(toDate)
              .append("\n\n");

        try (Connection connection =
                     DatabaseConnection.getConnection()) {

            /*
             * 1. TOTAL SESSIONS
             */
            String sessionCountSQL =
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_sessions "
                  + "FROM Sessions "
                  + "WHERE login_time >= ? "
                  + "AND login_time < ?";

            int totalSessions = 0;

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 sessionCountSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {

                        totalSessions =
                                resultSet.getInt("total_sessions");
                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * 2. TOTAL ACCESS ATTEMPTS
             */
            String accessCountSQL =
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_access "
                  + "FROM Access_Logs "
                  + "WHERE action_time >= ? "
                  + "AND action_time < ?";

            int totalAccessAttempts = 0;

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 accessCountSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {

                        totalAccessAttempts =
                                resultSet.getInt("total_access");
                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * 3. ALLOWED ACCESS
             */
            String allowedSQL =
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS allowed_count "
                  + "FROM Access_Logs "
                  + "WHERE action_time >= ? "
                  + "AND action_time < ? "
                  + "AND result = 'ALLOWED'";

            int allowedAccess = 0;

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 allowedSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {

                        allowedAccess =
                                resultSet.getInt("allowed_count");
                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * 4. DENIED ACCESS
             */
            String deniedSQL =
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS denied_count "
                  + "FROM Access_Logs "
                  + "WHERE action_time >= ? "
                  + "AND action_time < ? "
                  + "AND result = 'DENIED'";

            int deniedAccess = 0;

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 deniedSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {

                        deniedAccess =
                                resultSet.getInt("denied_count");
                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * 5. UNIQUE USERS
             */
            String userCountSQL =
                    "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) "
                  + "AS total_users "
                  + "FROM Access_Logs "
                  + "WHERE action_time >= ? "
                  + "AND action_time < ?";

            int totalUsers = 0;

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 userCountSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {

                        totalUsers =
                                resultSet.getInt("total_users");
                    }
                }
            }

            /*
             * 6. BUILD SUMMARY
             */
            report.append("REPORT SUMMARY\n");
            report.append("----------------------------------------\n");

            report.append("Total Sessions:       ")
                  .append(totalSessions)
                  .append("\n");

            report.append("Total Users Active:   ")
                  .append(totalUsers)
                  .append("\n");

            report.append("Total Access Attempts:")
                  .append(" ")
                  .append(totalAccessAttempts)
                  .append("\n");

            report.append("Allowed Access:       ")
                  .append(allowedAccess)
                  .append("\n");

            report.append("Denied Access:        ")
                  .append(deniedAccess)
                  .append("\n\n");

            /*
             * 7. CALCULATE ACCESS SUCCESS RATE
             */
            double successRate = 0;

            if (totalAccessAttempts > 0) {

                successRate =
                        ((double) allowedAccess
                        / totalAccessAttempts) * 100;
            }

            report.append("Access Success Rate:  ")
                  .append(String.format(
                          "%.2f", successRate))
                  .append("%\n\n");

            /*
             * 8. SESSION DETAILS
             */
            report.append("SESSION DETAILS\n");
            report.append("----------------------------------------\n");

            String sessionsSQL =
                    "SELECT "
                  + "s.session_id, "
                  + "s.user_id, "
                  + "u.full_name, "
                  + "s.login_time, "
                  + "s.logout_time "
                  + "FROM Sessions s "
                  + "JOIN Users u "
                  + "ON s.user_id = u.user_id "
                  + "WHERE s.login_time >= ? "
                  + "AND s.login_time < ? "
                  + "ORDER BY s.login_time";

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 sessionsSQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    boolean foundSession = false;

                    while (resultSet.next()) {

                        foundSession = true;

                        report.append("Session ID: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getInt(
                                      "session_id"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("User ID: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getInt(
                                      "user_id"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("User Name: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "full_name"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Login Time: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getTimestamp(
                                      "login_time"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Logout Time: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getTimestamp(
                                      "logout_time"))
                              .append("\n");

                        if (resultSet.getTimestamp(
                                "logout_time") == null) {

                            report.append("Status: ACTIVE\n");

                        } else {

                            report.append("Status: CLOSED\n");
                        }

                        report.append("----------------------------------------\n");
                    }

                    if (!foundSession) {

                        report.append(
                                "No sessions found for this period.\n");
                    }
                }
            }

            report.append("\n");

            /*
             * 9. ACCESS ACTIVITY DETAILS
             */
            report.append("ACCESS ACTIVITY\n");
            report.append("----------------------------------------\n");

            String activitySQL =
                    "SELECT "
                  + "al.log_id, "
                  + "u.full_name, "
                  + "al.session_id, "
                  + "o.operation_name, "
                  + "r.resource_name, "
                  + "al.target_record, "
                  + "al.action_time, "
                  + "al.result "
                  + "FROM Access_Logs al "
                  + "JOIN Users u "
                  + "ON al.user_id = u.user_id "
                  + "JOIN Operations o "
                  + "ON al.operation_id = o.operation_id "
                  + "JOIN Resources r "
                  + "ON al.resource_id = r.resource_id "
                  + "WHERE al.action_time >= ? "
                  + "AND al.action_time < ? "
                  + "ORDER BY al.action_time";

            try (PreparedStatement statement =
                         connection.prepareStatement(
                                 activitySQL)) {

                statement.setTimestamp(1, fromTimestamp);
                statement.setTimestamp(2, endTimestamp);

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                             statement.executeQuery()) {

                    boolean foundActivity = false;

                    while (resultSet.next()) {

                        foundActivity = true;

                        report.append("Log ID: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getInt(
                                      "log_id"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("User: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "full_name"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Session ID: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getInt(
                                      "session_id"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Operation: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "operation_name"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Resource: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "resource_name"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Target Record: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "target_record"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Action Time: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getTimestamp(
                                      "action_time"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("Result: ")
                              .append(
                                  resultSet.getString(
                                      "result"))
                              .append("\n");

                        report.append("----------------------------------------\n");
                    }

                    if (!foundActivity) {

                        report.append(
                                "No access activity found "
                              + "for this period.\n");
                    }
                }
            }

            report.append("\n");
            report.append("========================================\n");
            report.append("           END OF REPORT\n");
            report.append("========================================\n");

        } catch (SQLException e) {

            report.append("\nREPORT GENERATION ERROR\n");
            report.append("----------------------------------------\n");
            report.append(e.getMessage());
        }

        return report.toString();
    }
}

    /**
     * Creates new form ReportsForm
     */
    private UserAccount loggedInAccount;

    private PermissionCheckDAO permissionCheckDAO =
        new PermissionCheckDAO();
    
    public ReportsForm(UserAccount account) {

    initComponents();

    this.loggedInAccount = account;
    if (this.loggedInAccount != null) {

    int userId =
            this.loggedInAccount.getUserId();

    boolean canGenerateReport =
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Reports"
            );

    createReportButton.setEnabled(
            canGenerateReport
    );
    
    downloadPdfButton.setEnabled(
        canGenerateReport
    );
}

    javax.swing.JComponent fromDateEditor =
            (javax.swing.JComponent)
            fromDateChooser.getDateEditor().getUiComponent();

    fromDateEditor.addFocusListener(
            new java.awt.event.FocusAdapter() {

        @Override
        public void focusGained(
                java.awt.event.FocusEvent evt) {

            focusStatusLabel.setText(
                    "Focus Status: From date field is active"
            );
        }

        @Override
        public void focusLost(
                java.awt.event.FocusEvent evt) {

            if (fromDateChooser.getDate() == null) {

                focusStatusLabel.setText(
                        "Focus Status: Please select the From date"
                );

            } else {

                focusStatusLabel.setText(
                        "Focus Status: From date selected"
                );
            }
        }
    });

    javax.swing.JComponent toDateEditor =
            (javax.swing.JComponent)
            toDateChooser.getDateEditor().getUiComponent();

    toDateEditor.addFocusListener(
            new java.awt.event.FocusAdapter() {

        @Override
        public void focusGained(
                java.awt.event.FocusEvent evt) {

            focusStatusLabel.setText(
                    "Focus Status: To date field is active"
            );
        }

        @Override
        public void focusLost(
                java.awt.event.FocusEvent evt) {

            if (toDateChooser.getDate() == null) {

                focusStatusLabel.setText(
                        "Focus Status: Please select the To date"
                );

            } else {

                focusStatusLabel.setText(
                        "Focus Status: To date selected"
                );
            }
        }
    });
}

        new DashboardForm(loggedInAccount);

        dashboardForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_backButtonActionPerformed

    private void createReportButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_createReportButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        /*
     * Check permission again before generating the report.
     * This protects the operation even if the button is
     * triggered unexpectedly.
     */
    int userId = loggedInAccount.getUserId();

    boolean allowed =
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Reports"
            );

    if (!allowed) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Access denied. You do not have permission to generate reports.",
                "Permission Denied",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    /*
     * Get selected dates.
     */
    java.util.Date fromDate =
            fromDateChooser.getDate();

    java.util.Date toDate =
            toDateChooser.getDate();

    /*
     * Make sure both dates were selected.
     */
    if (fromDate == null || toDate == null) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please select both the From Date and To Date.",
                "Missing Date",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    /*
     * Make sure the date range is valid.
     */
    if (fromDate.after(toDate)) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The From Date cannot be after the To Date.",
                "Invalid Date Range",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    /*
     * Generate the report.
     */
    ReportDAO reportDAO =
            new ReportDAO();

    String report =
            reportDAO.generateReport(
                    fromDate,
                    toDate
            );

    /*
     * Display the generated report.
     */
    reportTextArea.setText(report);

    /*
     * Move the text area to the beginning.
     */
    reportTextArea.setCaretPosition(0);

    focusStatusLabel.setText(
            "Focus Status: Report generated successfully"
    );
    }//GEN-LAST:event_createReportButtonActionPerformed

    private void downloadPdfButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_downloadPdfButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (loggedInAccount == null) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "No logged-in account was found.",
                "Access Error",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int userId =
            loggedInAccount.getUserId();

    boolean allowed =
            permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
                    userId,
                    "VIEW",
                    "Reports"
            );

    if (!allowed) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Access denied. You do not have permission to download reports.",
                "Permission Denied",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    String reportText =
            reportTextArea.getText();

    if (reportText == null
            || reportText.trim().isEmpty()) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please generate a report first.",
                "No Report",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    javax.swing.JFileChooser fileChooser =
            new javax.swing.JFileChooser();

    fileChooser.setDialogTitle(
            "Save Access Permission System Report"
    );

    fileChooser.setSelectedFile(
            new java.io.File(
                    "Access_Permission_System_Report.pdf"
            )
    );

    int result =
            fileChooser.showSaveDialog(this);

    if (result != javax.swing.JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION) {

        return;
    }

    java.io.File selectedFile =
            fileChooser.getSelectedFile();

    String filePath =
            selectedFile.getAbsolutePath();

    if (!filePath.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf")) {

        filePath = filePath + ".pdf";
    }

    try {

        com.itextpdf.text.Document document =
                new com.itextpdf.text.Document(
                        com.itextpdf.text.PageSize.A4
                );

        com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter.getInstance(
                document,
                new java.io.FileOutputStream(filePath)
        );

        document.open();

        com.itextpdf.text.Font titleFont =
                new com.itextpdf.text.Font(
                        com.itextpdf.text.Font.FontFamily.HELVETICA,
                        16,
                        com.itextpdf.text.Font.BOLD
                );

        com.itextpdf.text.Font normalFont =
                new com.itextpdf.text.Font(
                        com.itextpdf.text.Font.FontFamily.HELVETICA,
                        10,
                        com.itextpdf.text.Font.NORMAL
                );

        com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph title =
                new com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph(
                        "ACCESS PERMISSION SYSTEM REPORT",
                        titleFont
                );

        title.setAlignment(
                com.itextpdf.text.Element.ALIGN_CENTER
        );

        document.add(title);

        document.add(
                new com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph(
                        " "
                )
        );

        String[] lines =
                reportText.split("\\r?\\n");

        for (String line : lines) {

            document.add(
                    new com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph(
                            line,
                            normalFont
                    )
            );
        }

        document.close();

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "PDF report created successfully.\n\n"
                + "Saved to:\n"
                + filePath,
                "PDF Created",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
        );

    } catch (Exception e) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The PDF could not be created.\n\n"
                + e.getMessage(),
                "PDF Error",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );
    }
    }//GEN-LAST:event_downloadPdfButtonActionPerformed

import com.itextpdf.text.Document;
import com.itextpdf.text.Paragraph;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfPTable;
import com.itextpdf.text.pdf.PdfWriter;
import java.awt.BorderLayout;
import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.Dimension;
import java.awt.Font;
import java.awt.GridLayout;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.ResultSetMetaData;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat;
import java.util.Date;
import javax.swing.BorderFactory;
import javax.swing.JButton;
import javax.swing.JFrame;
import javax.swing.JLabel;
import javax.swing.JOptionPane;
import javax.swing.JPanel;
import javax.swing.JScrollPane;
import javax.swing.JTable;
import javax.swing.JTextField;
import javax.swing.RowFilter;
import javax.swing.SwingConstants;
import javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel;
import javax.swing.table.TableRowSorter;

public class ReportWindow extends JFrame {

    private final String reportTitle;
    private final String reportDescription;
    private final String sql;

    private final JLabel totalValue = new JLabel("0");
    private final JLabel generatedValue = new JLabel("-");
    private final JLabel reportTypeValue = new JLabel("Database Report");
    private final JLabel recordCountLabel = new JLabel("Records: 0");

    private final JTextField searchField = new JTextField(22);
    private final JTable reportTable = new JTable();
    private final DefaultTableModel tableModel =
            new DefaultTableModel();
    private TableRowSorter<DefaultTableModel> sorter;

    public ReportWindow(String reportTitle,
                        String reportDescription,
                        String sql) {

        this.reportTitle = reportTitle;
        this.reportDescription = reportDescription;
        this.sql = sql;

        setTitle(reportTitle);
        setDefaultCloseOperation(JFrame.DISPOSE_ON_CLOSE);
        setMinimumSize(new Dimension(950, 600));
        setSize(1150, 720);
        setLocationRelativeTo(null);

        JPanel main = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 16));
        main.setBackground(new Color(245, 248, 252));
        main.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(20, 22, 20, 22));

        JPanel heading = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(5, 6));
        heading.setOpaque(false);

        JLabel systemName = new JLabel(
                "ACCESS & PERMISSION MANAGEMENT SYSTEM");
        systemName.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 12));
        systemName.setForeground(new Color(44, 91, 145));

        JLabel title = new JLabel(reportTitle);
        title.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 25));
        title.setForeground(new Color(20, 43, 75));

        JLabel description = new JLabel(reportDescription);
        description.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.PLAIN, 13));
        description.setForeground(new Color(85, 101, 122));

        JPanel titleGroup = new JPanel(new GridLayout(3, 1, 0, 5));
        titleGroup.setOpaque(false);
        titleGroup.add(systemName);
        titleGroup.add(title);
        titleGroup.add(description);

        heading.add(titleGroup, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        main.add(heading, BorderLayout.NORTH);

        JPanel body = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 14));
        body.setOpaque(false);

        JPanel summary = new JPanel(new GridLayout(1, 3, 14, 0));
        summary.setOpaque(false);
        summary.add(createSummaryCard(
                "TOTAL RECORDS", totalValue, new Color(30, 105, 190)));
        summary.add(createSummaryCard(
                "REPORT TYPE", reportTypeValue, new Color(112, 75, 190)));
        summary.add(createSummaryCard(
                "LAST REFRESHED", generatedValue, new Color(26, 143, 112)));

        JPanel tools = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(10, 0));
        tools.setOpaque(false);

        JPanel searchPanel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(8, 0));
        searchPanel.setOpaque(false);
        JLabel searchLabel = new JLabel("Search report:");
        searchLabel.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 12));
        searchPanel.add(searchLabel, BorderLayout.WEST);
        searchPanel.add(searchField, BorderLayout.CENTER);

        JButton refreshButton = createButton("Refresh");
        JButton exportButton = createButton("Export PDF");
        JButton closeButton = createButton("Close");

        JPanel actions = new JPanel(new java.awt.FlowLayout(
                java.awt.FlowLayout.RIGHT, 8, 0));
        actions.setOpaque(false);
        actions.add(refreshButton);
        actions.add(exportButton);
        actions.add(closeButton);

        tools.add(searchPanel, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        tools.add(actions, BorderLayout.EAST);

        reportTable.setModel(tableModel);
        reportTable.setRowHeight(27);
        reportTable.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.PLAIN, 12));
        reportTable.getTableHeader().setFont(
                new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 12));
        reportTable.getTableHeader().setBackground(
                new Color(225, 235, 247));
        reportTable.getTableHeader().setForeground(
                new Color(25, 48, 78));
        reportTable.setAutoResizeMode(JTable.AUTO_RESIZE_OFF);
        reportTable.setFillsViewportHeight(true);
        reportTable.setSelectionBackground(new Color(216, 233, 252));

        JScrollPane scrollPane = new JScrollPane(reportTable);
        scrollPane.setBorder(BorderFactory.createLineBorder(
                new Color(218, 226, 237)));

        JPanel tablePanel = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        tablePanel.setBackground(Color.WHITE);
        tablePanel.setBorder(BorderFactory.createCompoundBorder(
                BorderFactory.createLineBorder(new Color(218, 226, 237)),
                BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(12, 12, 12, 12)));
        tablePanel.add(scrollPane, BorderLayout.CENTER);

        recordCountLabel.setFont(
                new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 12));
        recordCountLabel.setForeground(new Color(67, 82, 103));
        tablePanel.add(recordCountLabel, BorderLayout.SOUTH);

        JPanel upper = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 14));
        upper.setOpaque(false);
        upper.add(summary, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        upper.add(tools, BorderLayout.SOUTH);

        body.add(upper, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        body.add(tablePanel, BorderLayout.CENTER);

        main.add(body, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        setContentPane(main);

        refreshButton.addActionListener(e -> loadData());
        closeButton.addActionListener(e -> dispose());
        exportButton.addActionListener(e -> exportPdf());

        searchField.addActionListener(e -> applySearch());

        searchField.getDocument().addDocumentListener(
                new javax.swing.event.DocumentListener() {
            @Override
            public void insertUpdate(
                    javax.swing.event.DocumentEvent e) {
                applySearch();
            }

            @Override
            public void removeUpdate(
                    javax.swing.event.DocumentEvent e) {
                applySearch();
            }

            @Override
            public void changedUpdate(
                    javax.swing.event.DocumentEvent e) {
                applySearch();
            }
        });

        loadData();
    }

    private JPanel createSummaryCard(
            String heading, JLabel value, Color accent) {

        JPanel card = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 10));
        card.setBackground(Color.WHITE);
        card.setBorder(BorderFactory.createCompoundBorder(
                BorderFactory.createMatteBorder(3, 0, 0, 0, accent),
                BorderFactory.createCompoundBorder(
                        BorderFactory.createLineBorder(
                                new Color(222, 229, 238)),
                        BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(13, 15, 13, 15))));

        JLabel label = new JLabel(heading);
        label.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 11));
        label.setForeground(new Color(90, 104, 123));

        value.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 20));
        value.setForeground(new Color(24, 47, 80));

        card.add(label, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        card.add(value, BorderLayout.CENTER);

        return card;
    }

    private JButton createButton(String text) {
        JButton button = new JButton(text);
        button.setFont(new Font("SansSerif", Font.BOLD, 12));
        button.setFocusPainted(false);
        button.setBackground(new Color(30, 103, 190));
        button.setForeground(Color.WHITE);
        button.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(
                10, 15, 10, 15));
        return button;
    }

    private void loadData() {
        tableModel.setRowCount(0);
        tableModel.setColumnCount(0);

        try (Connection connection =
                     DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {

            ResultSetMetaData metadata = result.getMetaData();
            int columns = metadata.getColumnCount();

            for (int i = 1; i <= columns; i++) {
                tableModel.addColumn(metadata.getColumnLabel(i));
            }

            while (result.next()) {
                Object[] row = new Object[columns];

                for (int i = 1; i <= columns; i++) {
                    row[i - 1] = result.getObject(i);
                }

                tableModel.addRow(row);
            }

            sorter = new TableRowSorter<>(tableModel);
            reportTable.setRowSorter(sorter);

            totalValue.setText(
                    String.valueOf(tableModel.getRowCount()));
            generatedValue.setText(
                    new SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm:ss").format(new Date()));
            recordCountLabel.setText(
                    "Records: " + tableModel.getRowCount());

            applySearch();

        } catch (SQLException ex) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "Could not load " + reportTitle + ":\n"
                            + ex.getMessage(),
                    "Report Error",
                    JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        }
    }

    private void applySearch() {
        if (sorter == null) {
            return;
        }

        String text = searchField.getText().trim();

        if (text.isEmpty()) {
            sorter.setRowFilter(null);
        } else {
            sorter.setRowFilter(
                    RowFilter.regexFilter(
                            "(?i)" + java.util.regex.Pattern.quote(text)));
        }

        recordCountLabel.setText(
                "Showing " + reportTable.getRowCount()
                + " of " + tableModel.getRowCount() + " records");
    }

    private void exportPdf() {
        if (reportTable.getRowCount() == 0) {
            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "There are no visible records to export.",
                    "Export PDF",
                    JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE);
            return;
        }

        javax.swing.JFileChooser chooser =
                new javax.swing.JFileChooser();

        String fileName = reportTitle.replaceAll(
                "[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "_") + ".pdf";

        chooser.setSelectedFile(new java.io.File(fileName));

        if (chooser.showSaveDialog(this)
                != javax.swing.JFileChooser.APPROVE_OPTION) {
            return;
        }

        Document document = new Document(
                com.itextpdf.text.PageSize.A4.rotate());

        try {
            PdfWriter.getInstance(
                    document,
                    new FileOutputStream(chooser.getSelectedFile()));

            document.open();

            document.add(new Paragraph(
                    "ACCESS & PERMISSION MANAGEMENT SYSTEM"));
            document.add(new Paragraph(reportTitle));
            document.add(new Paragraph(reportDescription));
            document.add(new Paragraph("Generated: "
                    + new SimpleDateFormat(
                            "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss").format(new Date())));
            document.add(new Paragraph(" "));
            
            PdfPTable pdfTable =
                    new PdfPTable(tableModel.getColumnCount());
            pdfTable.setWidthPercentage(100);

            for (int c = 0; c < tableModel.getColumnCount(); c++) {
                pdfTable.addCell(tableModel.getColumnName(c));
            }

            for (int r = 0; r < reportTable.getRowCount(); r++) {
                int modelRow = reportTable.convertRowIndexToModel(r);

                for (int c = 0; c < tableModel.getColumnCount(); c++) {
                    Object value = tableModel.getValueAt(modelRow, c);

                    pdfTable.addCell(
                            value == null ? "" : value.toString());
                }
            }

            document.add(pdfTable);
            document.close();

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "PDF exported successfully.",
                    "Export Complete",
                    JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE);

        } catch (Exception ex) {
            if (document.isOpen()) {
                document.close();
            }

            JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "PDF export failed:\n" + ex.getMessage(),
                    "Export Error",
                    JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE);
        }
    }
}

/**

 * @author yorekadev

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

public class Resource {

    private int resourceId;
    private String resourceName;
    private String description;

    public Resource() {
    }

    public Resource(int resourceId, String resourceName, String description) {
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
        this.resourceName = resourceName;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public int getResourceId() {
        return resourceId;
    }

    public void setResourceId(int resourceId) {
        this.resourceId = resourceId;
    }

    public String getResourceName() {
        return resourceName;
    }

    public void setResourceName(String resourceName) {
        this.resourceName = resourceName;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public void setDescription(String description) {
        this.description = description;
    }
}
/*
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Classes/Class.java to edit this template
 */
package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class ResourceDAO {

    public List<Resource> getAllResources() {

        List<Resource> resources = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT resource_id, resource_name, description "
                   + "FROM Resources";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Resource resource = new Resource(
                        resultSet.getInt("resource_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("resource_name"),
                        resultSet.getString("description")
                );

                resources.add(resource);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving resources: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return resources;
    }
    
        public boolean addResource(Resource resource) {

        String sql = "INSERT INTO Resources "
                   + "(resource_name, description) "
                   + "VALUES (?, ?)";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setString(1, resource.getResourceName());
            statement.setString(2, resource.getDescription());

            int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

            return rowsInserted > 0;

        } catch (SQLException e) {

            System.out.println("Error adding resource: "
                    + e.getMessage());

            return false;
        }
    }

    public boolean updateResource(Resource resource) {

        String sql = "UPDATE Resources "
                   + "SET resource_name = ?, description = ? "
                   + "WHERE resource_id = ?";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setString(1, resource.getResourceName());
            statement.setString(2, resource.getDescription());
            statement.setInt(3, resource.getResourceId());

            int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

            return rowsUpdated > 0;

        } catch (SQLException e) {

            System.out.println("Error updating resource: "
                    + e.getMessage());

            return false;
        }
    }

    public boolean deleteResource(int resourceId) {

        String sql = "DELETE FROM Resources "
                   + "WHERE resource_id = ?";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, resourceId);

            int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

            return rowsDeleted > 0;

        } catch (SQLException e) {

            System.out.println("Error deleting resource: "
                    + e.getMessage());

            return false;
        }
    }
}

    private void loadResources() {

    ResourceDAO resourceDAO = new ResourceDAO();

    java.util.List<Resource> resources =
            resourceDAO.getAllResources();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel)
                    resourcesTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (Resource resource : resources) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            resource.getResourceId(),
            resource.getResourceName(),
            resource.getDescription()
        });
    }
}

    private void addResourceButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_addResourceButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "CREATE",
        "Resources")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to create resources."
    );

    return;
}

AddResourceForm addResourceForm =
        new AddResourceForm(loggedInAccount);

addResourceForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_addResourceButtonActionPerformed

    private void backButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_backButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        DashboardForm dashboardForm =
        new DashboardForm(loggedInAccount);

        dashboardForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_backButtonActionPerformed

    private void refreshButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_refreshButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        loadResources();

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
        this,
        "Resources refreshed successfully."
);
    }//GEN-LAST:event_refreshButtonActionPerformed

    private void updateResourceButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_updateResourceButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Resources")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to update resources."
    );

    return;
}

int selectedRow = resourcesTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a resource to update."
    );

    return;
}

int resourceId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                resourcesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String resourceName =
        resourcesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

String description =
        resourcesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 2).toString();

EditResourceForm editResourceForm =
        new EditResourceForm(
                loggedInAccount,
                resourceId,
                resourceName,
                description
        );

editResourceForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_updateResourceButtonActionPerformed

    private void deleteResourceButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_deleteResourceButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "DELETE",
        "Resources")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to delete resources."
    );

    return;
}

int selectedRow = resourcesTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a resource to delete."
    );

    return;
}

int resourceId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                resourcesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String resourceName =
        resourcesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to delete "
                + resourceName + "?",
                "Confirm Delete",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

ResourceDAO resourceDAO = new ResourceDAO();

boolean success =
        resourceDAO.deleteResource(resourceId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Resource deleted successfully."
    );

    loadResources();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "The resource could not be deleted.\n\n"
            + "It may be referenced by one or more permissions.",
            "Delete Failed",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_deleteResourceButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

public class Role {

    private int roleId;
    private String roleName;
    private String description;

    public Role() {
    }

    public Role(int roleId, String roleName, String description) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
        this.roleName = roleName;
        this.description = description;
    }

    public int getRoleId() {
        return roleId;
    }

    public void setRoleId(int roleId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
    }

    public String getRoleName() {
        return roleName;
    }

    public void setRoleName(String roleName) {
        this.roleName = roleName;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public void setDescription(String description) {
        this.description = description;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class RoleAssignmentsReportForm extends ReportWindow {

    public RoleAssignmentsReportForm(UserAccount account) {
        super(
            "Role Assignments Report",
            "Review user-to-role assignments and users without roles.",
            "SELECT "
            + "u.user_id AS 'User ID', "
            + "u.full_name AS 'Full Name', "
            + "u.status AS 'User Status', "
            + "r.role_id AS 'Role ID', "
            + "r.role_name AS 'Role Name' "
            + "FROM Users u "
            + "LEFT JOIN User_Roles ur "
            + "ON ur.user_id = u.user_id "
            + "LEFT JOIN Roles r "
            + "ON r.role_id = ur.role_id "
            + "ORDER BY u.full_name, r.role_name"
        );
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RoleCheckDAO {

    public List<Role> getUserRoles(int userId) {

        List<Role> roles = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT r.role_id, r.role_name, r.description "
                   + "FROM Roles r "
                   + "INNER JOIN User_Roles ur "
                   + "ON r.role_id = ur.role_id "
                   + "WHERE ur.user_id = ?";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);

            try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

                while (resultSet.next()) {

                    Role role = new Role(
                            resultSet.getInt("role_id"),
                            resultSet.getString("role_name"),
                            resultSet.getString("description")
                    );

                    roles.add(role);
                }
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving user roles: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return roles;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class RoleConflict {

    private int roleId;
    private int conflictingRoleId;

    public RoleConflict() {
    }

    public RoleConflict(int roleId, int conflictingRoleId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
        this.conflictingRoleId = conflictingRoleId;
    }

    public int getRoleId() {
        return roleId;
    }

    public void setRoleId(int roleId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
    }

    public int getConflictingRoleId() {
        return conflictingRoleId;
    }

    public void setConflictingRoleId(int conflictingRoleId) {
        this.conflictingRoleId = conflictingRoleId;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RoleConflictDAO {

    public List<RoleConflict> getAllRoleConflicts() {

        List<RoleConflict> conflicts = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT role_id_1, role_id_2 "
                   + "FROM Role_Conflicts";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                RoleConflict conflict = new RoleConflict(
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id_1"),
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id_2")
                );

                conflicts.add(conflict);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving role conflicts: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return conflicts;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RoleDAO {

    public List<Role> getAllRoles() {

        List<Role> roles = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT role_id, role_name, description FROM Roles";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Role role = new Role(
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("role_name"),
                        resultSet.getString("description")
                );

                roles.add(role);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving roles: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return roles;
    }
    
    public boolean addRole(Role role) {

    String sql = "INSERT INTO Roles (role_name, description) "
               + "VALUES (?, ?)";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, role.getRoleName());
        statement.setString(2, role.getDescription());

        int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsInserted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error adding role: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean updateRole(Role role) {

    String sql = "UPDATE Roles "
               + "SET role_name = ?, description = ? "
               + "WHERE role_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, role.getRoleName());
        statement.setString(2, role.getDescription());
        statement.setInt(3, role.getRoleId());

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error updating role: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean deleteRole(int roleId) {

    String sql = "DELETE FROM Roles WHERE role_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, roleId);

        int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsDeleted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error deleting role: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class RoleHierarchy {

    private int parentRoleId;
    private int childRoleId;

    public RoleHierarchy() {
    }

    public RoleHierarchy(int parentRoleId, int childRoleId) {
        this.parentRoleId = parentRoleId;
        this.childRoleId = childRoleId;
    }

    public int getParentRoleId() {
        return parentRoleId;
    }

    public void setParentRoleId(int parentRoleId) {
        this.parentRoleId = parentRoleId;
    }

    public int getChildRoleId() {
        return childRoleId;
    }

    public void setChildRoleId(int childRoleId) {
        this.childRoleId = childRoleId;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RoleHierarchyDAO {

    public List<RoleHierarchy> getAllRoleHierarchies() {

        List<RoleHierarchy> hierarchies = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT parent_role_id, child_role_id "
                   + "FROM Role_Hierarchy";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                RoleHierarchy hierarchy = new RoleHierarchy(
                        resultSet.getInt("parent_role_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("child_role_id")
                );

                hierarchies.add(hierarchy);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving role hierarchy: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return hierarchies;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class RolePermission {

    private int roleId;
    private int permissionId;

    public RolePermission() {
    }

    public RolePermission(int roleId, int permissionId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
        this.permissionId = permissionId;
    }

    public int getRoleId() {
        return roleId;
    }

    public void setRoleId(int roleId) {
        this.roleId = roleId;
    }

    public int getPermissionId() {
        return permissionId;
    }

    public void setPermissionId(int permissionId) {
        this.permissionId = permissionId;
    }
}
/*

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RolePermissionDAO {

    public List<RolePermission> getAllRolePermissions() {

        List<RolePermission> rolePermissions = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT role_id, permission_id FROM Role_Permissions";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                RolePermission rolePermission = new RolePermission(
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("permission_id")
                );

                rolePermissions.add(rolePermission);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving role permissions: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return rolePermissions;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class RolePermissionsReportForm extends ReportWindow {

    public RolePermissionsReportForm(UserAccount account) {
        super(
            "Role Permissions Report",
            "Review which operations each role may perform on resources.",
            "SELECT "
            + "r.role_name AS Role, "
            + "res.resource_name AS Resource, "
            + "o.operation_name AS Operation, "
            + "p.description AS 'Permission Description' "
            + "FROM Roles r "
            + "JOIN Role_Permissions rp "
            + "ON rp.role_id = r.role_id "
            + "JOIN Permissions p "
            + "ON p.permission_id = rp.permission_id "
            + "JOIN Operations o "
            + "ON o.operation_id = p.operation_id "
            + "JOIN Resources res "
            + "ON res.resource_id = p.resource_id "
            + "ORDER BY r.role_name, res.resource_name, "
            + "o.operation_name"
        );
    }
}

    private void loadRoles() {

    RoleDAO roleDAO = new RoleDAO();

    java.util.List<Role> roles = roleDAO.getAllRoles();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) rolesTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (Role role : roles) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            role.getRoleId(),
            role.getRoleName(),
            role.getDescription()
        });
    }
}

    private void addRoleButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_addRoleButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "CREATE",
        "Roles")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to create roles."
    );

    return;
}
        
        AddRoleForm addRoleForm =
        new AddRoleForm(loggedInAccount);

        addRoleForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_addRoleButtonActionPerformed

    private void updateRoleButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_updateRoleButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Roles")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to update roles."
    );

    return;
}
        
        int selectedRow = rolesTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a role to update."
    );

    return;
}

int roleId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                rolesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String roleName =
        rolesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

String description =
        rolesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 2).toString();

Role role = new Role(
        roleId,
        roleName,
        description
);

EditRoleForm editRoleForm =
        new EditRoleForm(loggedInAccount, role);

editRoleForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_updateRoleButtonActionPerformed

    private void deleteRoleButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_deleteRoleButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "DELETE",
        "Roles")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to delete roles."
    );

    return;
}
        
        int selectedRow = rolesTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a role to delete."
    );

    return;
}

int roleId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                rolesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String roleName =
        rolesTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to delete "
                + roleName + "?",
                "Confirm Delete",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

RoleDAO roleDAO = new RoleDAO();

boolean success = roleDAO.deleteRole(roleId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Role deleted successfully."
    );

    loadRoles();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Failed to delete role."
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_deleteRoleButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

public class Session {

    private int sessionId;
    private int userId;
    private String loginTime;
    private String logoutTime;

    public Session() {
    }

    public Session(int sessionId, int userId, String loginTime, String logoutTime) {
        this.sessionId = sessionId;
        this.userId = userId;
        this.loginTime = loginTime;
        this.logoutTime = logoutTime;
    }

    public int getSessionId() {
        return sessionId;
    }

    public void setSessionId(int sessionId) {
        this.sessionId = sessionId;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String getLoginTime() {
        return loginTime;
    }

    public void setLoginTime(String loginTime) {
        this.loginTime = loginTime;
    }

    public String getLogoutTime() {
        return logoutTime;
    }

    public void setLogoutTime(String logoutTime) {
        this.logoutTime = logoutTime;
    }
}
/*
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Licenses/license-default.txt to change this license
 * Click nbfs://nbhost/SystemFileSystem/Templates/Classes/Class.java to edit this template
 */
package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class SessionDAO {

    public List<Session> getAllSessions() {

        List<Session> sessions = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT session_id, user_id, login_time, logout_time "
                   + "FROM Sessions";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                Session session = new Session(
                        resultSet.getInt("session_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("login_time"),
                        resultSet.getString("logout_time")
                );

                sessions.add(session);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving sessions: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return sessions;
    }
    
        public int createSession(int userId) {

        String sql = "INSERT INTO Sessions "
                   + "(user_id, login_time, logout_time) "
                   + "VALUES (?, NOW(), NULL)";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement =
                     connection.prepareStatement(
                             sql,
                             java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);

            int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

            if (rowsInserted > 0) {

                try (ResultSet resultSet =
                        statement.getGeneratedKeys()) {

                    if (resultSet.next()) {
                        return resultSet.getInt(1);
                    }
                }
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error creating session: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return 0;
    }
    
        public int getActiveSessionId(int userId) {

        String sql = "SELECT session_id "
                   + "FROM Sessions "
                   + "WHERE user_id = ? "
                   + "AND logout_time IS NULL "
                   + "ORDER BY login_time DESC "
                   + "LIMIT 1";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

            statement.setInt(1, userId);

            try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

                if (resultSet.next()) {
                    return resultSet.getInt("session_id");
                }
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving active session: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return 0;
    }
        
        public boolean closeSession(int sessionId) {

    String sql = "UPDATE Sessions "
               + "SET logout_time = NOW() "
               + "WHERE session_id = ? "
               + "AND logout_time IS NULL";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, sessionId);

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error closing session: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

    initComponents();

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    loadSessions();

    private void loadSessions() {

    SessionDAO sessionDAO = new SessionDAO();

    java.util.List<Session> sessions =
            sessionDAO.getAllSessions();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel)
                    sessionsTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (Session session : sessions) {

        String status;

        if (session.getLogoutTime() == null
                || session.getLogoutTime().isEmpty()) {

            status = "ACTIVE";

        } else {

            status = "CLOSED";
        }

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            session.getSessionId(),
            session.getUserId(),
            session.getLoginTime(),
            session.getLogoutTime(),
            status
        });
    }
}
