    private void closeSessionButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_closeSessionButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Sessions")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to close sessions."
    );

    return;
}

int selectedRow = sessionsTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a session to close."
    );

    return;
}

int sessionId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                sessionsTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String status =
        sessionsTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 4).toString();

if ("CLOSED".equals(status)) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "This session is already closed."
    );

    return;
}

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to close this session?",
                "Confirm Close Session",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

SessionDAO sessionDAO = new SessionDAO();

boolean success =
        sessionDAO.closeSession(sessionId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Session closed successfully."
    );

    loadSessions();

} else {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "The session could not be closed.",
            "Operation Failed",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
    );
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_closeSessionButtonActionPerformed

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

public class User {

    private int userId;
    private String fullName;
    private String email;
    private String status;

    public User() {
    }

    public User(int userId, String fullName, String email, String status) {
        this.userId = userId;
        this.fullName = fullName;
        this.email = email;
        this.status = status;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String getFullName() {
        return fullName;
    }

    public void setFullName(String fullName) {
        this.fullName = fullName;
    }

    public String getEmail() {
        return email;
    }

    public void setEmail(String email) {
        this.email = email;
    }

    public String getStatus() {
        return status;
    }

    public void setStatus(String status) {
        this.status = status;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

public class UserAccount {

    private int accountId;
    private int userId;
    private String username;
    private String passwordHash;
    private String accountStatus;
    private String lastLogin;

    public UserAccount() {
    }

    public UserAccount(int accountId, int userId, String username,
                       String passwordHash, String accountStatus,
                       String lastLogin) {
        this.accountId = accountId;
        this.userId = userId;
        this.username = username;
        this.passwordHash = passwordHash;
        this.accountStatus = accountStatus;
        this.lastLogin = lastLogin;
    }

    public int getAccountId() {
        return accountId;
    }

    public void setAccountId(int accountId) {
        this.accountId = accountId;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {
        this.userId = userId;
    }

    public String getUsername() {
        return username;
    }

    public void setUsername(String username) {
        this.username = username;
    }

    public String getPasswordHash() {
        return passwordHash;
    }

    public void setPasswordHash(String passwordHash) {
        this.passwordHash = passwordHash;
    }

    public String getAccountStatus() {
        return accountStatus;
    }

    public void setAccountStatus(String accountStatus) {
        this.accountStatus = accountStatus;
    }

    public String getLastLogin() {
        return lastLogin;
    }

    public void setLastLogin(String lastLogin) {
        this.lastLogin = lastLogin;
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class UserAccountDAO {

    public List<UserAccount> getAllUserAccounts() {

        List<UserAccount> accounts = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT account_id, user_id, username, password_hash, "
                   + "account_status, last_login FROM User_Accounts";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                UserAccount account = new UserAccount(
                        resultSet.getInt("account_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("username"),
                        resultSet.getString("password_hash"),
                        resultSet.getString("account_status"),
                        resultSet.getString("last_login")
                );

                accounts.add(account);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving user accounts: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return accounts;
    }
    
    public boolean disableAccount(int userId) {

    String sql = "UPDATE User_Accounts "
               + "SET account_status = 'DISABLED' "
               + "WHERE user_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, userId);

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error disabling account: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean createAccount(
        int userId,
        String username,
        String password,
        String accountStatus) {

    String passwordHash =
            PasswordUtil.hashPassword(password);

    String sql =
            "INSERT INTO User_Accounts "
            + "(user_id, username, password_hash, account_status) "
            + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";

    try (Connection connection =
                 DatabaseConnection.getConnection();

         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, userId);
        statement.setString(2, username);
        statement.setString(3, passwordHash);
        statement.setString(4, accountStatus);

        int rowsInserted =
                statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsInserted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println(
                "Error creating account: "
                + e.getMessage()
        );

        return false;
    }
}
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

public class UserAccountsReportForm extends ReportWindow {

    public UserAccountsReportForm(UserAccount account) {
        super(
            "User Accounts Report",
            "Review system users, login accounts and account status.",
            "SELECT "
            + "u.user_id AS 'User ID', "
            + "u.full_name AS 'Full Name', "
            + "u.email AS Email, "
            + "u.status AS 'User Status', "
            + "a.username AS Username, "
            + "a.account_status AS 'Account Status', "
            + "a.last_login AS 'Last Login' "
            + "FROM Users u "
            + "LEFT JOIN User_Accounts a "
            + "ON a.user_id = u.user_id "
            + "ORDER BY u.full_name"
        );
    }
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class UserDAO {

    public List<User> getAllUsers() {

        List<User> users = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT user_id, full_name, email, status FROM Users";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                User user = new User(
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getString("full_name"),
                        resultSet.getString("email"),
                        resultSet.getString("status")
                );

                users.add(user);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving users: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        return users;
    }
    
    public int addUser(User user) {

    String sql = "INSERT INTO Users (full_name, email, status) "
               + "VALUES (?, ?, ?)";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(
                         sql,
                         java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS)) {

        statement.setString(1, user.getFullName());
        statement.setString(2, user.getEmail());
        statement.setString(3, user.getStatus());

        int rowsInserted = statement.executeUpdate();

        if (rowsInserted > 0) {

            try (ResultSet resultSet =
                         statement.getGeneratedKeys()) {

                if (resultSet.next()) {
                    return resultSet.getInt(1);
                }
            }
        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println(
                "Error adding user: "
                + e.getMessage()
        );
    }

    return -1;
}
    
    public int getUserId(String email) {

    String sql =
            "SELECT user_id FROM Users WHERE email = ?";

    try (Connection connection =
                 DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, email);

        try (ResultSet resultSet =
                     statement.executeQuery()) {

            if (resultSet.next()) {
                return resultSet.getInt("user_id");
            }

        }

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println(
                "Error getting user ID: "
                + e.getMessage()
        );
    }

    return -1;
}
    
    public boolean updateUser(User user) {

    String sql = "UPDATE Users "
               + "SET full_name = ?, email = ?, status = ? "
               + "WHERE user_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setString(1, user.getFullName());
        statement.setString(2, user.getEmail());
        statement.setString(3, user.getStatus());
        statement.setInt(4, user.getUserId());

        int rowsUpdated = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsUpdated > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error updating user: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
    
    public boolean deleteUser(int userId) {

    String sql = "DELETE FROM Users WHERE user_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, userId);

        int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsDeleted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error deleting user: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

        this.roleId = roleId;
    }

    public int getUserId() {
        return userId;
    }

    public void setUserId(int userId) {

    }

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */

import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class UserRoleDAO {

    public List<UserRole> getAllUserRoles() {

        List<UserRole> userRoles = new ArrayList<>();

        String sql = "SELECT user_id, role_id FROM User_Roles";

        try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
             PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
             ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {

            while (resultSet.next()) {

                UserRole userRole = new UserRole(
                        resultSet.getInt("user_id"),
                        resultSet.getInt("role_id")
                );

                userRoles.add(userRole);
            }

        } catch (SQLException e) {
            System.out.println("Error retrieving user roles: "
                    + e.getMessage());
        }

        return userRoles;
    }
    
    public boolean removeUserRole(int userId, int roleId) {

    String sql = "DELETE FROM User_Roles "
               + "WHERE user_id = ? "
               + "AND role_id = ?";

    try (Connection connection = DatabaseConnection.getConnection();
         PreparedStatement statement =
                 connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {

        statement.setInt(1, userId);
        statement.setInt(2, roleId);

        int rowsDeleted = statement.executeUpdate();

        return rowsDeleted > 0;

    } catch (SQLException e) {

        System.out.println("Error removing user role: "
                + e.getMessage());

        return false;
    }
}
}

package accesspermissionmanagementsystem;

/**
 *
 * @author yorekadev
 */
public class UsersForm extends javax.swing.JFrame {

    /**
     * Creates new form UsersForm
     */
    
    private UserAccount loggedInAccount;
    
    private PermissionCheckDAO permissionCheckDAO =
        new PermissionCheckDAO();
    
    public UsersForm(UserAccount account) {

    initComponents();

    this.loggedInAccount = account;

    loadUsers();

    findButton.addActionListener(e -> findUser());

    firstButton.addActionListener(e -> goToFirstUser());

    previousButton.addActionListener(e -> goToPreviousUser());

    nextButton.addActionListener(e -> goToNextUser());
}
    
    private void loadUsers() {

    UserDAO userDAO = new UserDAO();

    java.util.List<User> users = userDAO.getAllUsers();

    javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel model =
            (javax.swing.table.DefaultTableModel) usersTable.getModel();

    model.setRowCount(0);

    for (User user : users) {

        model.addRow(new Object[]{
            user.getUserId(),
            user.getFullName(),
            user.getEmail(),
            user.getStatus()
        });
    }
}
    
    private void findUser() {

    if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
            loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
            "VIEW",
            "Users")) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Access denied. You do not have permission to view users.",
                "Permission Denied",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    String search =
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showInputDialog(
                    this,
                    "Enter User ID, name, or email:",
                    "Find User",
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.QUESTION_MESSAGE
            );

    if (search == null || search.trim().isEmpty()) {
        return;
    }

    search = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    for (int row = 0; row < usersTable.getRowCount(); row++) {

        String userId =
                usersTable.getValueAt(row, 0).toString();

        String fullName =
                usersTable.getValueAt(row, 1).toString();

        String email =
                usersTable.getValueAt(row, 2).toString();

        if (userId.toLowerCase().contains(search)
                || fullName.toLowerCase().contains(search)
                || email.toLowerCase().contains(search)) {

            usersTable.setRowSelectionInterval(row, row);

            usersTable.scrollRectToVisible(
                    usersTable.getCellRect(row, 0, true)
            );

            return;
        }
    }

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "No user was found matching: " + search,
            "User Not Found",
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
    );
}


private void goToFirstUser() {

    if (usersTable.getRowCount() == 0) {
        return;
    }

    usersTable.setRowSelectionInterval(0, 0);

    usersTable.scrollRectToVisible(
            usersTable.getCellRect(0, 0, true)
    );
}


private void goToPreviousUser() {

    int selectedRow =
            usersTable.getSelectedRow();

    if (selectedRow == -1) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please select a user first.",
                "No User Selected",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    if (selectedRow == 0) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "You are already at the first user.",
                "First User",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int previousRow =
            selectedRow - 1;

    usersTable.setRowSelectionInterval(
            previousRow,
            previousRow
    );

    usersTable.scrollRectToVisible(
            usersTable.getCellRect(previousRow, 0, true)
    );
}


private void goToNextUser() {

    int selectedRow =
            usersTable.getSelectedRow();

    if (selectedRow == -1) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Please select a user first.",
                "No User Selected",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int lastRow =
            usersTable.getRowCount() - 1;

    if (selectedRow == lastRow) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "You are already at the last user.",
                "Last User",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.INFORMATION_MESSAGE
        );

        return;
    }

    int nextRow =
            selectedRow + 1;

    usersTable.setRowSelectionInterval(
            nextRow,
            nextRow
    );

    usersTable.scrollRectToVisible(
            usersTable.getCellRect(nextRow, 0, true)
    );
}

    private void backButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_backButtonActionPerformed

        DashboardForm dashboardForm =
        new DashboardForm(loggedInAccount);

        dashboardForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_backButtonActionPerformed

    private void addUserButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_addUserButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
            loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
            "CREATE",
            "Users")) {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "Access denied. You do not have permission to create users."
        );

        return;
    }
        
        AddUserForm addUserForm =
        new AddUserForm(loggedInAccount);

        addUserForm.setVisible(true);

        this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_addUserButtonActionPerformed

    private void updateUserButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_updateUserButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "UPDATE",
        "Users")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to update users."
    );

    return;
}
        
            int selectedRow = usersTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a user to update."
    );

    return;
}

int userId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String fullName =
        usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

String email =
        usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 2).toString();

String status =
        usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 3).toString();

User user = new User(
        userId,
        fullName,
        email,
        status
);

EditUserForm editUserForm =
        new EditUserForm(loggedInAccount, user);

editUserForm.setVisible(true);

this.dispose();
    }//GEN-LAST:event_updateUserButtonActionPerformed

    private void deleteUserButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_deleteUserButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        
        if (!permissionCheckDAO.hasPermission(
        loggedInAccount.getUserId(),
        "DELETE",
        "Users")) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Access denied. You do not have permission to delete users."
    );

    return;
}
        
        int selectedRow = usersTable.getSelectedRow();

if (selectedRow == -1) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "Please select a user to delete."
    );

    return;
}

int userId =
        Integer.parseInt(
                usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 0).toString()
        );

String fullName =
        usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 1).toString();

int confirmation =
        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                this,
                "Are you sure you want to delete "
                + fullName + "?",
                "Confirm Delete",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION
        );

if (confirmation != javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    return;
}

UserDAO userDAO = new UserDAO();

boolean success = userDAO.deleteUser(userId);

if (success) {

    javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
            this,
            "User deleted successfully."
    );

    loadUsers();

} else {

    int deactivateConfirmation =
            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showConfirmDialog(
                    this,
                    "This user cannot be permanently deleted "
                    + "because they have related records in the system.\n\n"
                    + "Would you like to deactivate "
                    + fullName + " instead?",
                    "Delete User",
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_NO_OPTION,
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
            );

    if (deactivateConfirmation
        == javax.swing.JOptionPane.YES_OPTION) {

    User user = new User(
            userId,
            fullName,
            usersTable.getValueAt(selectedRow, 2).toString(),
            "INACTIVE"
    );

    boolean deactivated =
            userDAO.updateUser(user);

    if (deactivated) {

        UserAccountDAO userAccountDAO =
                new UserAccountDAO();

        boolean accountDisabled =
                userAccountDAO.disableAccount(userId);

        if (accountDisabled) {

            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "User has been deactivated successfully.\n"
                    + "Their login account has also been disabled."
            );

        } else {

            javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                    this,
                    "User was deactivated, but the login account "
                    + "could not be disabled.",
                    "Warning",
                    javax.swing.JOptionPane.WARNING_MESSAGE
            );
        }

        loadUsers();

    } else {

        javax.swing.JOptionPane.showMessageDialog(
                this,
                "The user could not be deleted or deactivated.",
                "Operation Failed",
                javax.swing.JOptionPane.ERROR_MESSAGE
        );
    }
}
}
    }//GEN-LAST:event_deleteUserButtonActionPerformed

    private void lastButtonActionPerformed(java.awt.event.ActionEvent evt) {//GEN-FIRST:event_lastButtonActionPerformed
        // TODO add your handling code here:
        if (usersTable.getRowCount() == 0) {
        return;
    }

    int lastRow =
            usersTable.getRowCount() - 1;

    usersTable.setRowSelectionInterval(
            lastRow,
            lastRow
    );

    usersTable.scrollRectToVisible(
            usersTable.getCellRect(lastRow, 0, true)
    );
    }//GEN-LAST:event_lastButtonActionPerformed
